 - Evooo1Bot targets devices from major brands exploiting known vulnerabilities.
 - The malware features capabilities for credential theft and launching DDoS attacks.

A study has found that waist circumference and waist-to-hip ratio are better predictors of cardiovascular disease risk than body mass index (BMI). The research shows that individuals classified as normal weight by BMI may still have higher cardiovascular risk due to elevated central adiposity, suggesting a need to reconsider how cardiovascular risk is assessed.

 - Abdominal measurements can indicate heart disease risk more accurately than BMI.
 - Individuals with normal BMI may still be at risk due to fat distribution.

Source: https://www.acc.org/about-acc/press-releases/2026/08/11/14/59/abdominal-fat-predicts-heart-disease-risk-better-than-bmi

Category: Other
Importance: 8
Recent research has shown that waist circumference and waist-to-hip ratio are more accurate predictors of cardiovascular disease risk than body mass index (BMI). This study analyzed data from over 260,000 individuals and revealed that a significant portion of those classified as normal weight by BMI had elevated central adiposity, which is linked to higher cardiovascular risk. The findings suggest a need for reevaluation of how cardiovascular risk is assessed, advocating for a broader consideration of fat distribution metrics.

 - Waist circumference (WC) and waist-to-hip ratio (WHR) better predict cardiovascular disease risk compared to BMI.
 - Individuals with normal BMI but high WC or WHR are associated with significantly greater cardiovascular risks.

Source: https://www.acc.org/about-acc/press-releases/2026/08/11/14/59/abdominal-fat-predicts-heart-disease-risk-better-than-bmi

A new study has challenged a 60-year-old theory regarding the social structures of ant colonies, revealing that the genetic system of haplodiploidy is not the primary driver of eusociality in insects. Researchers examined evolutionary relationships among nearly 69,000 insect species and found that complex social systems occur independently of haplodiploidy, suggesting that many factors influence the development of such communities.

 - The study reveals that haplodiploidy does not universally explain the emergence of complex ant colonies.
 - Multiple factors including environmental conditions and social structures also contribute to the evolution of eusocial behavior in insects.

Category: Business
Importance: 5
Donald Trump recently claimed credit for a drop in prescription drug prices in the United States, attributing the decline to his administration. However, experts and reports indicate that the significant price reductions are primarily due to policies enacted under President Joe Biden, particularly the Inflation Reduction Act, which allows Medicare to negotiate drug prices. Data shows that prescription drug prices fell by 3.1% in July, marking the lowest levels in 60 years.

 - Experts attribute the decrease primarily to Joe Biden's policies.
 - Prescription drug prices fell by 3.1% in July, the lowest in 60 years.

Galileo's principles of physics establish the impossibility of human giants due to the limitations of strength and mass scaling in relation to size. Larger structures do not proportionally support larger weights, leading to catastrophic failures, which also provides insight into the evolution of various animal sizes. Consequently, the characteristics of different species are inherently linked to their sizes, influenced by evolutionary forces that shape body plans for survival.

 - Galileo argued that human giants could not exist as increased size leads to disproportionate weight and strength.
 - His principles explain the evolutionary adaptations in animals of varying sizes, demonstrating how physical laws govern existence.

Source: https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/388-years-ago-galileo-worked-out-why-human-giants-cant-exist-and-explained-a-law-of-nature/

---


Category: Other
Importance: 5
Galileo's work from 388 years ago explains why giant humans cannot exist based on principles of physics and scaling. He argued that as size increases, the strength of bones and structures does not scale in the same way as mass, resulting in physical limitations that make giant creatures impossible. This insight has implications for understanding the sizes of animals throughout evolution.

 - Galileo's observations show that as size increases, the cross-sectional strength of bones scales with the square of linear dimensions, while mass scales with the cube.
 - The inability of larger beings, like giants, to support their own weight illustrates fundamental laws of physics that govern biological form and function.

Source: https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/388-years-ago-galileo-worked-out-why-human-giants-cant-exist-and-explained-a-law-of-nature/
